# Settings compatibility and field index

This is a source-derived **candidate index**, not a semantically certified serialized schema. It records 926 public field/property candidates from configuration-related source files, including original declarations and locations. Some are transient, nested or serializer-ignored. At implementation time use Roslyn/reflection, JsonIgnore/default attributes, configuration constructors and UI bindings to establish the final authoritative editable/serialized set. Retain the original candidate list to show reconciliation decisions.

All actual settings are in scope. For every real setting, capture source type, JSON key, default, range/validation, UI label/control, group inheritance, side effects, migration rule, and corresponding acceptance fixture. Do not claim option parity from a generic JSON editor. Preserve unknown values where safe; invalid or nonportable values produce an import report.

## Migration contract

Preserve source JSON enum names and numeric/flag values; group-specific defaults; task inheritance; naming tokens; uploader selection; source string encodings; and effect preset IDs. Keychain replaces local secret storage. Windows-encrypted secrets, drive/UNC paths and keycodes need explicit treatment. Settings writes are atomic and migrations operate on copies/backups. The readable declaration below is evidence, not permission to execute its contents.
